register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef ensure_requirement_referents(tv: TaskView) -> int:\n  \"\"\"Materialize requirement referents referenced by derived SQL output.\"\"\"\n  rows = tv.query(\n      \"\"\"\n      SELECT DISTINCT requirement_id\n      FROM monitoring_requirement_candidate\n      \"\"\"\n  )\n  count = 0\n  for row in rows:\n f\"LIMIT_ID={match.group(1)};LIMIT_VALUE_ID={match.group(2)}\"\n          if match\n          else req_id\n      )\n      tv.add_referent(\n          req_id,\n          label=req_id,\n          grounding=[\n              Grounding(\n                  GroundingKind.WORLD,\n                  \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n                  detail,\n              )\n          ],\n      )\n register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n   AND am.active = 1\n+        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n+          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n+        \"\"\",\n+        inputs=[\n+            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n+            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n+            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n+        ],\n+    )\n+    tv.register_derivation(\n+        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n+        sql=f\"\"\"\n+        SELECT\n+            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n+            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n+            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n+            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n+            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n+            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS              ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n+            END AS requirement_kind\n+        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n+        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n+        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n+          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n+          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n+        \"\"\",\n+        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n+    )\n+    tv.register_derivation(\n+        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n+        sql=f\"\"\"\n+        SELECT\n+            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n+            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n+            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n+            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n+            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n+            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n+            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n+            CASE\n+                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n+                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n+                ELSE 0\n+            END AS has_numeric_value\n+        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n+        INNER JOIN inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def ensure_requirement_referents(tv: TaskView) -> int:\n+  \"\"\"Materialize requirement referents referenced by derived SQL output.\"\"\"\n+  rows = tv.query(\n+      \"\"\"\n+      SELECT DISTINCT requirement_id\n+      FROM monitoring_requirement_candidate\n+      \"\"\"\n+  )\n+  count = 0\n+  for row in f\"LIMIT_ID={match.group(1)};LIMIT_VALUE_ID={match.group(2)}\"\n+          if match\n+          else req_id\n+      )\n+      tv.add_referent(\n+          req_id,\n+          label=req_id,\n+          grounding=[\n+              Grounding(\n+                  GroundingKind.WORLD,\n+                  \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n+                  detail,\n+              )\n+          ],\n+      )\n+      count += 1\n+  return count\n+\n+\n+def run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n+    tv.run_derivation(\n+        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n+        completeness=Completeness(\n+  )\n+    tv.run_derivation(\n+        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n+        completeness=Completeness(\n+            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n+            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n+            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n+        ),\n+    )\n+    tv.run_derivation(\n+        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n+        completeness=Completeness(\n+            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n+            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n+            basis=\"All register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef ensure_requirement_referents(tv: TaskView) -> int:\n  \"\"\"Materialize requirement referents referenced by derived SQL output.\"\"\"\n  rows = tv.query(\n      \"\"\"\n      SELECT DISTINCT requirement_id\n      FROM monitoring_requirement_candidate\n      \"\"\"\n  )\n  count = 0\n  for row in rows:\n CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef ensure_requirement_referents(tv: TaskView) -> int:\n  \"\"\"Materialize requirement referents referenced by derived SQL output.\"\"\"\n  rows = tv.query(\n      \"\"\"\n      SELECT DISTINCT requirement_id\n      FROM monitoring_requirement_candidate\n      \"\"\"\n  )\n  count = 0\n  for row in rows:\n      req_id = row[\"requirement_id\"]\n      if not req_id:\n          continue\n      match = re.fullmatch(r\"req:(.+):(.+)\", req_id)\n      detail = (\n          f\"LIMIT_ID={match.group(1)};LIMIT_VALUE_ID={match.group(2)}\"\n          if match\n          else req_id\n      )\n      tv.add_referent(\n          req_id,\n          label=req_id,\n          grounding=[\n              Grounding(\n                  GroundingKind.WORLD,\n                  \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n                  detail,\n              )\n          ],\n      )\n CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n overlapping FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n relation[\"row_count\"]\n        if mode == \"BASE\":\n            base += rows\n        elif name in {\n            \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n            \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n            \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        }:\n            candidate += rows\n    return base, candidate\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    if register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope limit schedule rows with frequency overlapping FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"FY2025 measurements with absent numeric value or NODI code.\",\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef count_tuples(tv: TaskView) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    base = 0\n    candidate = 0\n    for relation in tv.describe()[\"relations\"]:\n        mode = relation[\"mode\"]\n        name = relation[\"name\"]\n        rows = relation[\"row_count\"]\n        if mode == \"BASE\":\n            base += rows\n        elif name in {\n            \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n            \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n            \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        }:\n            candidate += rows\n    return base, candidate\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    if 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS                 row_number,\n                        f\"DMR_EVENT_ID={event_id};DMR_VALUE_ID={value_id}\",\n                    )\n                ],\n            )\n\n    return stats\n\n\ndef register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope limit schedule rows with frequency overlapping FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"FY2025 measurements with absent numeric value or NODI code.\",\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef count_tuples(tv: TaskView) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    base = 0\n    candidate = 0\n    for relation in tv.describe()[\"relations\"]:\n        mode = relation[\"mode\"]\n        name = relation[\"name\"]\n        rows = relation[\"row_count\"]\n        if mode == \"BASE\":\n            base += rows\n        elif name in {\n            \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n            \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n            \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        }:\n            candidate += rows\n    return base, candidate\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    if            f\"loaded {compile_stats['limit_rows_read'] - compile_stats['limit_rows_deduped']} unique limit rows.\"\n            ),\n            (\n                f\"dmr_measurements.csv: \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n                    {\n                        \"limit_row\": lref,\n                        \"calendar_month\": month,\n                        \"active\": active,\n                    },\n                    grounding=[\n                        csv_row_grounding(\n                            \"sources/structured/permit_limits.csv\",\n                            row_number,\n                            f\"LIMIT_ID={limit_id};LIMIT_VALUE_ID={limit_value_id};column={month}={flag}\",\n                        )\n                    ],\n                )\n                stats[\"active_month_tuples\"] += 1\n\n    with DMR_PATH.open(encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\") as handle:\n        for row_number, row in enumerate(csv.DictReader(handle), start=2):\n            stats[\"dmr_rows_read\"] += 1\n            permit = row[\"EXTERNAL_PERMIT_NMBR\"].strip()\n            if permit not in FACILITIES:\n                continue\n            event_id = dmr_value_nbr = parse_real(row[\"DMR_VALUE_NMBR\"])\n            if dmr_value_nbr == ABSENT_REAL:\n                stats[\"dmr_absent_numeric\"] += 1\n\n            tv.add_referent(\n                 row_number,\n                        f\"DMR_EVENT_ID={event_id};DMR_VALUE_ID={value_id}\",\n                    )\n                ],\n            )\n\n    return stats\n\n\ndef register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope limit schedule rows with frequency overlapping FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n            basis=\"FY2025 measurements with absent numeric value or NODI code.\",\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef count_tuples(tv: TaskView) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    base = 0\n    candidate = 0\n    for relation in tv.describe()[\"relations\"]:\n        mode = relation[\"mode\"]\n        name = relation[\"name\"]\n        rows = relation[\"row_count\"]\n        if mode == \"BASE\":\n            base += rows\n        elif name in {\n            \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n            \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n            \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        }:\n            candidate += rows\n    return base, candidate\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    if           f\"permit_limits.csv: read {compile_stats['limit_rows_read']} rows, \"\n                f\"deduplicated {compile_stats['limit_rows_deduped']} duplicate LIMIT_ID/LIMIT_VALUE_ID rows, \"\n                f\"loaded {compile_stats['limit_rows_read'] - compile_stats['limit_rows_deduped']} unique limit rows.\"\n            ),\n            (\n                f\"dmr_measurements.csv: register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            basis=\"All )\n\n\ndef count_tuples(tv: TaskView) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    base = 0\n    candidate = 0\n    for relation in tv.describe()[\"relations\"]:\n        mode = relation[\"mode\"]\n        name = register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row_id,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            basis=\"All )\n\n\ndef count_tuples(tv: TaskView) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    base = 0\n    candidate = 0\n    for relation in tv.describe()[\"relations\"]:\n        mode = relation[\"mode\"]\n        name = register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    absent = repr(ABSENT_REAL)\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            m.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end\n        FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = m.permit_nbr\n        WHERE m.monitoring_period_end >= '{FY2025_START}'\n          AND m.monitoring_period_end <= '{FY2025_END}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"reported_dmr_measurement\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        WITH measurement_month AS (\n            SELECT\n                m.*,\n                CASE CAST(strftime('%m', m.monitoring_period_end) AS INTEGER)\n                    WHEN 1 THEN 'JAN'\n                    WHEN 2 THEN 'FEB'\n                    WHEN 3 THEN 'MAR'\n                    WHEN 4 THEN 'APR'\n                    WHEN 5 THEN 'MAY'\n                    WHEN 6 THEN 'JUN'\n                    WHEN 7 THEN 'JUL'\n                    WHEN 8 THEN 'AUG'\n                    WHEN 9 THEN 'SEP'\n                    WHEN 10 THEN 'OCT'\n                    WHEN 11 THEN 'NOV'\n                    WHEN 12 THEN 'DEC'\n                END AS period_month\n            FROM reported_dmr_measurement m\n        )\n        SELECT\n            mm.measurement_id AS measurement_id,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row_id,\n            mm.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            mm.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            mm.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            mm.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            'equijoin(permit_nbr,perm_feature_nbr,parameter_code);date_overlap(monitoring_period_end,limit_begin_date,limit_end_date);active_month('\n                || mm.period_month || ')' AS join_basis\n        FROM measurement_month mm\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_schedule_row l\n            ON l.permit_nbr = mm.permit_nbr\n           AND l.perm_feature_nbr = mm.perm_feature_nbr\n           AND l.parameter_code = mm.parameter_code\n        INNER JOIN permit_limit_active_month am\n            ON am.limit_row_id = l.limit_row_id\n           AND am.calendar_month = mm.period_month\n           AND am.active = 1\n        WHERE mm.monitoring_period_end >= l.limit_begin_date\n          AND mm.monitoring_period_end <= l.limit_end_date\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            \"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            \"permit_limit_active_month\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            'req:' || l.limit_id || ':' || l.limit_value_id AS requirement_id,\n            l.limit_row_id AS limit_row_id,\n            l.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            l.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            l.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code AS limit_freq_of_analysis_code,\n            l.optional_monitoring_flag AS optional_monitoring_flag,\n            CASE\n                WHEN trim(l.dmr_comment_text) = '' THEN 'standing_schedule'\n                ELSE 'comment_text_present'\n            END AS requirement_kind\n        FROM permit_limit_schedule_row l\n        INNER JOIN in_scope_facility f ON f.permit_nbr = l.permit_nbr\n        WHERE trim(l.limit_freq_of_analysis_code) != ''\n          AND l.limit_begin_date <= '{FY2025_END}'\n          AND l.limit_end_date >= '{FY2025_START}'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"permit_limit_schedule_row\", \"in_scope_facility\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"no_ordinary_numeric_result_measurement\",\n        sql=f\"\"\"\n        SELECT\n            f.measurement_id AS measurement,\n            f.measurement_id AS expectation,\n            m.permit_nbr AS permit_nbr,\n            m.perm_feature_nbr AS perm_feature_nbr,\n            m.parameter_code AS parameter_code,\n            m.monitoring_period_end AS monitoring_period_end,\n            m.nodi_code AS nodi_code,\n            CASE\n                WHEN m.dmr_value_nbr != {absent}\n                 AND trim(m.nodi_code) = '' THEN 1\n                ELSE 0\n            END AS has_numeric_value\n        FROM fy2025_in_scope_measurement f\n        INNER JOIN reported_dmr_measurement m ON m.measurement_id = f.measurement_id\n        WHERE m.dmr_value_nbr = {absent}\n           OR trim(m.nodi_code) != ''\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\", \"reported_dmr_measurement\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"fy2025_in_scope_measurement\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"All in-scope facility DMR rows with monitoring_period_end in FY2025.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"measurement_limit_candidate_join\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"reported_dmr_measurement\",\n            basis=\"Equijoin and date/month overlap over all reported measurements.\",\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"monitoring_requirement_candidate\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,\n            universe=\"permit_limit_schedule_row\",\n            basis=\"All )\n\n\ndef count_tuples(tv: TaskView) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    base = 0\n    candidate = 0\n    for relation in tv.describe()[\"relations\"]:\n        mode = relation[\"mode\"]\n        name =
